           │ river bank but the ferryman crosses the river or stays on the other bank. We wish to know how the ferryman can transport all goods to the other side, │

           │ river bank but the ferryman crosses the river or stays on the other bank. We wish to know how the ferryman can transport all goods to the other side, │            
           │ without any conflicts occurring. Build a model in which the states represent which goods are at which side of the river. Your model will need to have │            
           │ variables representing the location of the ferryman, the goat, the cabbage and the wolf and a variable that indicates what is being carried in the    │            
           │ ferry. Define a goal state in which all goods are on the other side of the river. Write a property which checks whether this goal state is reachable  │

           │ river bank but the ferryman crosses the river or stays on the other bank. We wish to know how the ferryman can transport all goods to the other side, │            
           │ without any conflicts occurring. Build a model in which the states represent which goods are at which side of the river. Your model will need to have │            
           │ variables representing the location of the ferryman, the goat, the cabbage and the wolf and a variable that indicates what is being carried in the    │            
           │ ferry. Define a goal state in which all goods are on the other side of the river. Write a property which checks whether this goal state is reachable  │

[01:51:44] ╭──────────────────────────────────────────────────────── 🧠 Original Response: ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╮                 utils.py:43
           │ class Module:                                                                                                                         │                            
           │     """An abstract class to represent a UCLID5 module."""                                                                             │                            
           │                                                                                                                                       │                            
           │     def types(self):                                                                                                                  │                            
           │         """Defines the type declarations."""                                                                                          │                            
           │         self.side = Enum(2)  # 0 represents left side, 1 represents right side                                                        │                            
           │         self.ferryman = self.side                                                                                                     │                            
           │         self.goat = self.side                                                                                                         │                            
           │         self.cabbage = self.side                                                                                                      │                            
           │         self.wolf = self.side                                                                                                         │                            
           │         self.carry = Enum(4)  # 0 represents empty, 1 represents ferryman, 2 represents goat, 3 represents cabbage, 4 represents wolf │                            
           │                                                                                                                                       │                            
           │     def locals(self):                                                                                                                 │                            
           │         """Defines the local variables and their types."""                                                                            │                            
           │         self.goal = Boolean()                                                                                                         │                            
           │                                                                                                                                       │                            
           │     def init(self):                                                                                                                   │                            
           │         """Defines how variables are initialized."""                                                                                  │                            
           │         self.ferryman = 0                                                                                                             │                            
           │         self.goat = 0                                                                                                                 │                            
           │         self.cabbage = 0                                                                                                              │                            
           │         self.wolf = 0                                                                                                                 │                            
           │         self.carry = 0                                                                                                                │                            
           │         self.goal = False                                                                                                             │                            
           │                                                                                                                                       │                            
           │     def next(self):                                                                                                                   │                            
           │         """Defines the transition relation."""                                                                                        │                            
           │         if self.ferryman == 0:  # Ferryman is on the left side                                                                        │                            
           │             if self.carry == 0:  # Ferryman is carrying nothing                                                                       │                            
           │                 if self.wolf == 0 and self.cabbage == 0:  # Wolf and cabbage are on the left side                                     │                            
           │                     # Ferryman takes the Wolf to the right side                                                                       │                            
           │                     self.wolf = 1                                                                                                     │                            
           │                     self.ferryman = 1                                                                                                 │                            
           │                     self.carry = 4                                                                                                    │                            
           │                 elif self.wolf == 0 and self.goat == 0:  # Wolf and goat are on the left side                                         │                            
           │                     # Ferryman takes the Goat to the right side                                                                       │                            
           │                     self.goat = 1                                                                                                     │                            
           │                     self.ferryman = 1                                                                                                 │                            
           │                     self.carry = 2                                                                                                    │                            
           │                 else:                                                                                                                 │                            
           │                     # Ferryman is not carrying anything or no conflict, move the Ferryman alone to the right side                     │                            
           │                     self.ferryman = 1                                                                                                 │                            
           │                     self.carry = 1                                                                                                    │                            
           │             else:                                                                                                                     │                            
           │                 # Ferryman is carrying one of the goods and moves back to the right side                                              │                            
           │                 self.ferryman = 1                                                                                                     │                            
           │         else:  # Ferryman is on the right side                                                                                        │                            
           │             if self.carry != 0:                                                                                                       │                            
           │                 # Ferryman successfully reached the other side with goods                                                             │                            
           │                 self.goal = True                                                                                                      │                            
           │ ```                                                                                                                                   │

           │                     # Ferryman is not carrying anything or no conflict, move the Ferryman alone to the right side                     │                            
           │                     self.ferryman = 1                                                                                                 │                            
           │                     self.carry = 1                                                                                                    │

           │     def types(self):                                                                                                                                  │            
           │         """(Optional) Defines the type declarations.                                                                                                  │

           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │            
           │         def types(self):                                                                                                                              │            
           │             self.T = BitVector(8)                                                                                                                     │

           │         """                                                                                                                                           │            
           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │            
           │                                                                                                                                                       │            
           │     def locals(self):                                                                                                                                 │            
           │         """(Optional) Defines the local variables and their types.                                                                                    │            
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an 8-bit variable x                                                                         │            
           │         and an integer variable y:                                                                                                                    │            
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │

           │             self.x = BitVector(8)                                                                                                                     │            
           │             self.y = Integer()                                                                                                                        │            
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │            
           │         """                                                                                                                                           │            
           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │            
           │                                                                                                                                                       │            
           │     def inputs(self):                                                                                                                                 │            
           │         """(Optional) Defines the input variables and their types.                                                                                    │            
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an input variable x,                                                                        │            
           │         which is an array of 8-bit bitvectors indexed by 2-bit bitvectors:                                                                            │

           │             self.x = Array(BitVector(2), BitVector(8))                                                                                                │            
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │            
           │         """                                                                                                                                           │            
           │         pass                                                                                                                                          │            
           │                                                                                                                                                       │            
           │     def outputs(self):                                                                                                                                │            
           │         """(Optional) Defines the output variables and their types.                                                                                   │            
           │         For example, the following implementation defines an output variable y,                                                                       │            
           │         which is a real number:                                                                                                                       │            
           │         ```                                                                                                                                           │
